Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the rings framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 574. The resulting operator achieves bound O(450/n^1).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the rings space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 574 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 450. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the rings object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 416. The moduli space has dimension at most 670 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the structural decomposition decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_416(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 416. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^670(S, F) is trivial. QED.
